- **wizard**: A **wizard** is a bash script that walks a human, step by step, through a manual procedure that's tedious to do by hand and tedious to re-explain to an AI every time. It opens each URL, says exactly what to click and copy, captures the values, writes them where they belong (`.env`, GitHub secrets), confirms at every stage, and shows how many stages are left. It might configure third-party services ([svssdeva/agentic-skills](https://github.com/svssdeva/agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/engineering/wizard) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **wizard**: Tạo một wizard bash tương tác dẫn người dùng đi qua những bước chỉ họ mới làm được. Dùng khi cấp phát hạ tầng, thiết lập credential hoặc secret cho CI, đi qua một dashboard bên thứ ba lạ lẫm, hoặc chạy một lần migration hay chuyển đổi (cutover). Không dùng cho những bước agent tự làm được. Câu kích hoạt: "làm wizard hướng dẫn mình setup", "tạo script từng bước để lấy API key", "make a setup wizard", "walk me through setting up the secrets". - **wk-workstyle-error-handling**: Error-handling code — catch/rescue/except, error returns, raise/throw. - **wmt-mt-meta-eval**: Evaluates machine translation metrics by measuring their alignment with human judgments at segment and system levels. Use when the user wants to benchmark on WMT Test Sets, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Pearson correlation. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/wmt-mt-meta-eval) · ★3)
- **wmt-slt-22-eval**: Evaluates the capability of sign language to text translation systems on low-resource datasets.
